Credo landing pages: QA report

Updated 24 Sept 2026: all 30 pages checked in detail. The audit at the top lists the issues common to every page, then the issues found on each page, each with a recommended solution in Webflow. The cross-browser check (Playwright on 3 engines and 8 devices, axe, Lighthouse) was re-run on 24 Sept and follows below, each finding with its Webflow fix.
Audit and Webflow solutions (22–24 Sept)Cross-browser check (24 Sept)

Audit and recommended solutions in Webflow, 22–24 Sept

Part A: issues on every page, by priority. Part B: the issues found on each page and the values the general fixes need on it.

How this report is organised. Part A lists the issues that apply to every page, by priority. Part B has one section per page: the issues found only on that page, plus the values the general fixes need on it (its meta description, its H1 accent word, its citations, and whether the sticky bar covers its form). Every item states the problem, its status on 24 Sep, and a recommended solution in Webflow: where to click and what to change. All 30 pages were checked in detail on 24 Sep: the 26 with a prototype, and the four without one.

Before you start: the 26 pages are not linked. The 26 pages with a prototype carry identical custom code (apart from the slug) and identical sections, but these are separate copies, not Components. Every change to a section or to page custom code therefore has to be made 26 times. Two one-off changes make every later fix a single edit:

  1. Move the custom code that all pages share into Site settings → Custom code (Head code / Footer code) and leave only page-specific lines in Page settings → Custom code. See W1.
  2. Turn repeated sections (form card, trust strip, bottom CTA, sticky bar, phone-swap embed) into Components (right-click the section in the Navigator → Create component), with component properties for any text that changes per page.

Staging is NEW Credo Legal Landing Pages (6a5be784cc1cab7ef8e99233, enroll.credolegal.com). Production is a separate site, Credo Legal Landing Pages (6903ad8d7b4674d0123baecd, start.credolegal.com). The findings below were checked against staging as published at 12:55 UTC on 24 Sep. The footer follows the separate footer handoff, not the prototype, so it is only checked for firm details.

A · Issues on every page — Critical

G9 · Lead form posts PII to formspree

Priority Critical (privacy) · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. Webflow's form settings post the form to https://formspree.io/f/xzzyzzwj, a third-party relay that receives name, phone, email, ZIP, state and debt details.

Recommended solution in Webflow. Decide the destination first: CRM, a first-party endpoint, or formspree with a DPA in place. Then select the Form Block (#gtmform) → Settings panel → Form settings → Action:

  • For Webflow's own form handling, clear Action (and Method). Submissions then land in Site settings → Forms, and can be sent to the CRM through a webhook or an integration (Zapier/Make).
  • For a first-party endpoint, enter its URL in Action with Method POST.

Either way, keep Turnstile. Afterwards, check that the success page still shows (the form is submitted by the site head script with gtmForm.submit()).

A · Issues on every page — High

G3 · Meta descriptions: prototype text, now to be shortened

Priority High · Status 24 Sep Done as first specified (the texts equal the prototype on 26/26). Decision 24 Sep: shorten them to ≤160 characters.

Problem. Because they copy the prototype exactly, all 26 descriptions run 162–249 characters and fail the ≤160 check.

Recommended solution in Webflow. For each page: Pages panel → gear icon (Page settings) → SEO settings → Meta description: paste the approved text given in that page's section below. Under Open Graph settings, tick Same as SEO description so og:description (and the Twitter description Webflow derives from it) stays identical. Publish. On request the 26 texts can be written in one bulk update through the Webflow API instead.

About the approved texts. They are used for the meta description, og:description and twitter:description, and written into the prototype first. None carries the NY disclaimer snippet: it cannot fit in 160 characters, and every page already has the attorney-advertising disclaimer in its footer. The texts drop two claims from the prototype descriptions:

  • "Every illegal call is up to $1,000": the FDCPA caps statutory damages at $1,000 per lawsuit (§ 1692k(a)(2)(A)), not per call.
  • "Recent CFPB rule changes" on medical-debt-credit-report-removal: to our knowledge the 2025 CFPB medical-debt reporting rule was vacated by a federal court in July 2025; to confirm.

G10 · No consent line at the form

Priority High (compliance) · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. The details step ends at "Back / Get a free case evaluation →" with no TCPA/SMS consent line and no "not legal advice" line. The privacy policy is linked from the footer only.

Recommended solution in Webflow. Inside the form's details step (#formstep3), directly under the submit button, add a Paragraph. Give it a new class form-consent (13px, colour #5b6470, which passes contrast on white), and paste the consent text that Credo's counsel approves, with a link to the privacy policy. If counsel requires express consent, add a required Checkbox above the button instead. Do it once in a Component (see "Before you start") so all 26 pages get it.

G32 · Session recording and chat on a PII form — to verify

Priority High (verify) · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. Mouseflow (page code) and Microsoft Clarity (loaded through GTM) both record sessions. The Tidio chat and Optibase A/B testing load on every page. Not yet checked: whether the recorders mask the form inputs (name, phone, email, ZIP, the situation text).

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  1. Select the Form Block → Settings panel → Custom attributes: add data-clarity-mask = true, which makes Clarity mask everything inside the form.
  2. Mouseflow: turn on input masking in the Mouseflow project's privacy settings, or remove Mouseflow from the page head code if Clarity is enough. Running two recorders is redundant.
  3. Record Tidio and Optibase in the privacy review next to G9.

G20 · The Meta click ID (fbclid) is never captured

Priority High · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. The UTM script in the site head stores fbclig (typo) instead of fbclid, and the form has no fbclid field. In a test, gclid reached a cookie and a form field, and fbclid reached neither.

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  1. Site settings → Custom code → Head code: in the UTM persistence block, change 'fbclig' to 'fbclid' in utmKeys and in fieldMapping, and fbclig: to fbclid: in the click-tracking data. Save.
  2. In the Designer, select the hidden gclid field in the form and copy-paste it (Cmd+C / Cmd+V). In the copy's Settings, set Name and ID to fbclid and remove the fs-queryparam-name attribute, or set it to fbclid.
  3. Publish, then test with ?fbclid=test123: the value must appear in the fbclid field.

F1 · Phone field opens the letter keyboard (cross-browser check; still present in the 24 Sep re-run)

Priority High · Status 24 Sep Open (confirmed in Webflow: the phone input is inputType: text)

Recommended solution in Webflow. Select the Phone Number field (#n-phone-number) → Settings panel → Text field settings → Type: Phone. Webflow then outputs type="tel", and phones show the number pad. The formatting script keeps working.

G18 · Statute citations change case

Priority High · Status 24 Sep Open · Pages all 30 (179 citations, in the Rights section and in the problem cards)

Problem. Every citation label style (mjfdcpaboxtext, mjfdcpaboxtext Copy, … Copy Copy, … Copy Copy Copy) has letter case set to uppercase, so subsection letters display as capitals: "§ 1692e(2)" shows as "§ 1692E(2)", "§ 1692c(a)(1)" as "§ 1692C(A)(1)", "FRCP 60(b)" as "60(B)". The text stored in Webflow is correct.

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  1. Select a citation label → Style panel → Typography → More type options → Capitalize: None (the "—" icon). Repeat for each class in the family. There are about 10 near-duplicate mjfdcpaboxtext Copy Copy classes, so merge them into one class, e.g. citation, and apply it everywhere.
  2. Word labels that should stay upper-case-looking ("Civil right", "Due process") are then shown as typed: retype them in the case the prototype shows.

The citations on each page are listed in its page section below, so they can be checked after the fix.

G19 · On mobile, the sticky call bar covers the Continue button

Priority High · Status 24 Sep Open · phones only, during business hours

Problem. The phone-swap Embed script creates the fixed bar .sticky-call-button ("Is your debt situation becoming urgent? Call Now", 72px tall, z-index 9999999) only when the screen is ≤768px wide and it is business hours (Mon–Fri 9 AM–9 PM, Sat 9 AM–5 PM ET). What it covers, tested with the clock set inside business hours:

  • Step 1, first screen: where Continue lands depends on how many lines each page's H1 and sub-headline wrap to. Across all 30 pages, the bar covers Continue on first view on 21 pages: 19 on iPhone 15 (393×852) and/or 390×844, and 2 on Pixel 7 (412×915). On the other 9 pages it clears the button at every tested size. On iPhone SE (375×667) and Galaxy S8 (360×740), Continue always sits below the first screen. Each page section below says whether the bar covers Continue on that page.
  • Steps 2–3 (the pop-up form): the bar also sits on top of the pop-up (z-index 10000). The pop-up has 150px of bottom padding, so its buttons can be scrolled clear (steps 2 and 3 were walked on all 30 pages at iPhone 15 size, and both buttons were tappable at the bottom). The bar still covers fields while the visitor scrolls through them.
  • Desktop, laptop and tablets wider than 768px: no bar (it isn't created, and CSS hides it above 768px). At 768×1024 it sits well below Continue. Outside business hours there's no bar at any width.

The rendered prototype has no sticky bar (its stylesheet has a .sticky-cta rule, but this page variant never adds one to the page). The bar is worth keeping: in GA4 it's the most-clicked call element (17 clicks on enroll since 17 Sep against 3 and 2 for the other call links, and 54 on start).

Recommended solution in Webflow. Keep the bar, but show it only when no form is on screen: while the step-1 form card is in view it is hidden; once the visitor scrolls past the form it appears; while the pop-up form (steps 2–3) is open it is hidden; when the pop-up closes it returns.

  1. Starting state. The script creates the bar, so style it in the same Embed's <style>: add opacity:0; pointer-events:none; transition:opacity .2s; to .sticky-call-button, plus a rule .sticky-call-button.is-visible { opacity:1; pointer-events:auto; }.
  2. Show and hide on scroll. Webflow interactions can't target the bar, because it doesn't exist in the Designer; the script creates it after the page loads. So add a few lines to the same Embed script, right after the bar is appended: an IntersectionObserver that watches the step-1 form card (the wrapper around #gtmform) and toggles is-visible on the bar (add it when the card leaves the screen, remove it when the card is back). Watch the form card, not the whole hero section: on phones the hero also contains the photo below the form, so the bar would stay hidden longer than needed.
  3. Hide during the pop-up. The form script already adds the class mj-popup-open to the page body while the pop-up is open. Add one CSS line in Site settings → Custom code → Head code: body.mj-popup-open .sticky-call-button { display: none !important; }.
  4. Check on a 390×844 phone during business hours: first screen, Continue free and no bar; after scrolling past the form, the bar appears; tap Continue, the pop-up opens and the bar disappears; close the pop-up, the bar returns.

G25 · The phone number isn't visible in the mobile header

Priority High · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. The prototype's mobile header shows the tracked number and a "FREE REVIEW →" button. On live, the call link (#navcta, inside nav-menu-wrapper) sits inside the collapsing Nav Menu, so on phones it's hidden behind the hamburger.

Recommended solution in Webflow. In the Navigator, copy the call link and paste the copy outside the Nav Menu element, next to the Menu Button, so it doesn't collapse. Show the copy only on tablet and mobile (Display none on desktop) and hide the original on those breakpoints. Give the copy its own id (e.g. mobilenavcta) and add that id to the phone-swap script's list of links, so it shows the tracked number. Add a small "Free review →" button that links to the form (#herosec).

L1 · multi-step.js throws on every page and calls a staging server (cross-browser check; still present in the 24 Sep re-run)

Priority High · Status 24 Sep Open, cause found

Problem. multi-step.js (Videsigns' library) is loaded from each page's footer code. It looks for form[data-form="multistep"], and no element on these pages has a data-form attribute, so it throws ("multistepForm is null") in every engine. The form works because its steps, validation and submit run from the site-wide head script. The POST to videsigns-staging.co.uk/counter comes from inside the library itself (line 2500).

Recommended solution in Webflow. Page settings → Custom code → Before body tag: delete <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/videsigns/webflow-tools@latest/multi-step.js"></script> (all 26 pages, or once if the code is moved to Site settings per W1). Publish, then walk the form (continue → details → back) to confirm the steps still work.

L2 · Main content appears late on phones (LCP 9–15 s, Lighthouse 37–56 in the 24 Sep re-run)

Priority High · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. Lighthouse's top saving is unused JavaScript (about 3–4 s). The page loads GTM twice (G30), multi-step.js (L1), Font Awesome with no icons in use, Inputflow, Finsweet, Optibase, Mouseflow, Tidio and, through GTM, Clarity, Meta, Bing and Nextdoor pixels.

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  1. Delete what isn't used: multi-step.js (L1), the duplicate GTM tag (G30), and the Font Awesome <link> in page head code (G31).
  2. Site settings → Custom code: load Tidio only after the visitor interacts (wrap it in a script that injects it after the first scroll or tap), or remove it if chat isn't staffed.
  3. Keep one session recorder (G32).
  4. Site settings → Publishing → Advanced publishing options: turn on minify HTML, CSS and JS.
  5. Re-run Lighthouse on staging and compare against 37–56.

A · Issues on every page — Medium

G6 · Schema: wrong address and URL, no FAQPage

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Open (also carries the schema part of G8)

Problem. Webflow's own schema field is empty. The JSON-LD sits in each page's head code: a LegalService whose url is the homepage, whose logo and image come from the production site's asset store, and whose address is 2151 W Hillsboro Blvd, Deerfield Beach, FL 33442. There is no FAQPage, and twitter:site is the placeholder @handle.

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  1. Upload the red Credo logo to this site: Assets panel → Upload. Copy its URL.
  2. Site settings → Custom code → Head code: add one site-wide LegalService block with name "Credo Legal Services, P.C.", url "https://start.credolegal.com/", the new logo URL for logo/image, address 1 Liberty Street, Suite 4010, New York, NY 10006, telephone +1-212-461-4026, and the existing sameAs links (confirm each profile is live).
  3. Page settings → Custom code → Inside head tag on each page: delete the old LegalService block; add a FAQPage block built from that page's own FAQ questions and answers.
  4. In the same page head code, delete <meta name="twitter:site" content="@handle">, or replace it with Credo's real X/Twitter handle if one is in use.

G21 · Call-click tracking — needs discussion

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Open, needs discussion

Problem.

  • (a) The site-head tracker that posts call clicks to credo.debtfixer.co/getUTM.php listens only for ids clicktocall, clicktocall1 and clicktocall2. None of them exist, so it never fires.
  • (b) GA4 does record call clicks through GTM, but each click is an event named after its link text or number (on enroll since 17 Sep: "IS YOUR DEBT SITUATION BECOMING URGENT?" 17, "(347) 523-4568" 3, "(720) 414-1751" 2). None of them is a key event.

Recommended solution in Webflow (once agreed).

  1. Site settings → Custom code → Head code: in the click-tracking block, change const ids = ["clicktocall", "clicktocall1", "clicktocall2"] to the real call-link ids ["navcta", "herocta", "footercta", "bfootercta"]. Give the sticky bar link an id first (select it → Settings → ID stickycta) and add that too. If this tracker is no longer used, delete the whole block.
  2. Outside Webflow, in GTM: replace the per-text click events with one event call_click (parameter: the number) fired on tel: links, and mark it as a key event in GA4.

F2 · Dropdown options live in a script, not in Webflow (G17, remainder)

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Mostly fixed. Alternative Phone and Address are gone, ZIP is required (5 digits), and the six debt types match the prototype.

Problem. The count and security dropdowns show the prototype's options only because a site-head script rebuilds them on load. Webflow still stores the old ones (1-2 / 3-5 / 6+; "Secured with collateral / Unsecured / Don't Know"). If the script fails, visitors get the old options. The debt-stage options also differ from the prototype in capitalisation and wording ("Behind On Payments", "Being Sued/Served Papers").

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  1. Select the Select debts field (#debts) → Settings → Select field settings → Options. Set: placeholder "Select debts" (value empty), then "1 debt", "2–3 debts", "4–5 debts", "6 or more".
  2. Select Debt Security (#Debt-Security) → Options: placeholder "Select debt security", then "Unsecured (no collateral)", "Secured (collateral)", "Not sure".
  3. Select Select Debt Stage → Options: "Select debt stage", then "Behind on payments", "In collections", "Being sued / served papers", "Judgment entered", "Wage garnishment".
  4. Site settings → Custom code → Head code: delete the block headed G17: DROPDOWN OPTIONS TO MATCH PROTOTYPE.

F3 · The amount slider has no label (cross-browser check; still present in the 24 Sep re-run)

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Open

Recommended solution in Webflow. Select the range input #mjSlider → Settings → Custom attributes: add aria-label = How much do you currently owe in total? (the medical credit-report page uses its own question wording). If the slider sits inside an Embed, add the attribute in the embed's HTML instead.

G27 · The form card differs from the prototype, and "or call" fails contrast

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. The live card has a thick black border, while the prototype has a soft shadow and no border. The prototype puts "Or call (number)" under the card in dark mono text. Live puts it inside the card as faint grey "OR CALL" (class callustext Copy, #c8cdd6 on white, 1.6:1).

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  1. Select the form card wrapper → Style panel → Borders: remove the border. Effects → Box shadows: add a soft shadow (e.g. 0 1px 3px rgba(17,20,24,.08) and 0 8px 24px rgba(17,20,24,.06)).
  2. In the Navigator, drag the block that holds "or call" (div-block-37) out of the card, to just below it.
  3. Select callustext Copy → Typography: colour #111418 (or the secondary grey #6a7688 for the words), font Inconsolata, Capitalize None. The number link stays black and bold.

G22 · Intro paragraph in Title Case

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Open · all 30 pages

Recommended solution in Webflow. Select the "What we do" paragraph (class Text Block 16) → Style panel → Typography → More type options → Capitalize: None. One change fixes every page that uses the class.

G23 · The H1 accent word isn't red

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. Each prototype H1 has one red accent word ("Is a Creditor Suing You?"). The prototype builds it as prefix<em>accent</em>suffix from the page's h1 array, coloured by one CSS rule. Live H1s are plain text. The bottom CTA has a native <em>decision</em>, but a page-footer script also regex-searches #ctaheading for "decision" and wraps it in an inline color:#ff6b73 span. That script is redundant, depends on the keyword, runs after load (so the colour flashes in) and uses a different red.

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  1. Create one class for the accent: double-click a heading, select one word and choose Span in the text formatting toolbar that appears (this wraps the selection in a <span>); give that span a new class accent, and set colour #c92028 and font style Normal.
  2. On each page, double-click the H1, select the accent word and wrap it in a span with class accent. The accent word for each page is given in its page section below.
  3. Bottom CTA: replace the italic "decision" with the same accent span (or give the heading's italic text style Normal and colour #c92028).
  4. Page settings → Custom code → Before body tag: delete the script that looks for ctaheading and "decision".

G24 · The mobile type scale is much larger than the prototype's

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. At 390px the H1 is 52px (prototype 32px) and section headlines are 42px (prototype 26px). Wrapped form questions have a 44px line-height, which leaves a large gap between lines ("How much do you currently owe in / total?").

Recommended solution in Webflow. Switch to the Mobile portrait breakpoint (and check Mobile landscape):

  • Heading 46 (H1): font size 32px, line height 1.05.
  • mjthankyouheading and mjthankyouheading Copy (section headlines): 26px, line height 1.2.
  • Heading 47 (form questions): line height 1.3 on all breakpoints. The 44px value comes from the page's "heading hierarchy fix" <style> in the page head code; change line-height: 44px there to 1.3 for .heading-47.

G26 · Text is lighter and greyer than the prototype

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. The body tag still has Webflow's defaults (Arial 14px, #333), so any text without its own class falls back to them. Styled text uses #333 or weight 300 where the prototype uses ink #111418 at weight 400 (eyebrows and card titles 600 instead of 500).

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  1. Variables panel: create colour variables ink #111418 and muted #5b6470.
  2. Select Body (All pages) in the Style panel selector → font Hanken Grotesk, colour ink.
  3. On classes set to weight 300 (e.g. Text Block 16, the card and lede texts), set 400. On eyebrow classes (mjthankyousteps Copy) and card titles, set 600. Where a class hard-codes #333, point it at ink.

G16 · CTA labels

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. The pages use the prototype's four labels ("Get a free case evaluation", "See if you qualify, free", "Speak to an attorney now", "Continue"). G16 asked for two.

Recommended solution in Webflow. Once the two labels are agreed, make the CTA button a Component with a text property (right-click → Create component). Set the text once and reuse the component in every section, so the labels stay consistent on all 26 pages.

L3 · Contrast failures (cross-browser check; still present in the 24 Sep re-run)

Priority Medium · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. The worst is "or call" (callustext Copy, #c8cdd6 on white, 1.6:1; see G27). Other text in #8590a0 on white is 3.2:1 (e.g. "Fill in the form below…" and the mjfdcpaboxtext Copy family).

Recommended solution in Webflow. Create a colour variable secondary #6a7688 (passes 4.5:1 on white). Then in the Style panel, set every class that uses #8590a0 or #c8cdd6 on a white background to that variable. #c8cdd6 on the dark footer and bottom CTA passes and can stay.

A · Issues on every page — Low

G30 · GTM is included twice

Priority Low · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. Page head code has a bare <script async src="…gtm.js?id=GTM-PTLMPVGH"> plus the standard GTM snippet for the same container. In a test the container loaded and started once, so it is redundant rather than harmful.

Recommended solution in Webflow. Page settings → Custom code → Inside head tag: delete the line <script async="" src="https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtm.js?id=GTM-PTLMPVGH"></script> and keep the block marked <!-- Google Tag Manager -->. Better still, move that block to Site settings → Custom code → Head code once and remove it from all 26 pages (W1).

F4 · Duplicate element ids (cross-browser check; still present in the 24 Sep re-run)

Priority Low · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. step-1, step-2 and step-3 appear 3 times each, formstep2 twice, and Debt-Type 6 times. Labels and scripts that look up an id reach only the first match.

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  • Debt-type checkboxes: select each checkbox → Settings → ID: debt-type-credit-card, debt-type-medical, and so on. Keep Name Debt-Type on all six, so the submission and the validation script still work.
  • The step markers (step-1/2/3) come from copies of the progress-bar Embed. In each extra copy, rename them (e.g. step-1-b) or switch to classes.
  • For the second formstep2 element: Settings → ID, rename to something unique. Leave the one used by the form's step logic in the site head script alone.

G28 · The bottom CTA is centred, the prototype is left-aligned

Priority Low · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. The bottom CTA's Embed <style> sets .bottom-cta { text-align: center }. The prototype is left-aligned with a two-line headline.

Recommended solution in Webflow. Open the Embed inside section-35 bottom-cta and change text-align: center to left. Give the headline (Heading 39 Copy) a max width of about 14em so it wraps onto two lines. The same Embed has the typo in L6; fix both in one edit.

G29 · "Week 1" step tag in mixed case

Priority Low · Status 24 Sep Open · 21/30 pages (listed in the page sections)

Recommended solution in Webflow. In How it works, double-click "Week 1" and type "WEEK 1", or give it the same class as its siblings ("DAY 0", "BY DEADLINE").

L4 · Text smaller than 12px on phones (cross-browser check; still present in the 24 Sep re-run)

Priority Low · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. The form step labels ("YOUR DEBT", "YOUR SITUATION", "YOUR DETAILS", class .stl) are 10px on phones, and the rights tags (mjfdcpaboxtext) are 11px.

Recommended solution in Webflow. The step labels are styled in the progress-bar Embed's <style>: change .stl { font-size: 10px } in the mobile media query (and 11px in the base rule) to 12px. Select mjfdcpaboxtext → font size 12px.

L5 · Links too small to tap (cross-browser check; still present in the 24 Sep re-run)

Priority Low · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem. The call links #herocta and #footercta are 15px tall, the footer link-block-5 phone links 20px, and the BBB profile link 18px (target: at least 24px).

Recommended solution in Webflow. Give these links vertical padding so each is at least 24px tall (44px is better on phones): select the link → Style panel → Spacing: padding top and bottom 6–12px, Display inline-block. For the BBB link, add padding to its link block.

L6 · The page is wider than the screen (cross-browser check; still present in the 24 Sep re-run)

Priority Low · Status 24 Sep Open, cause found

Problem. The bottom CTA's Embed <style> says poition:relative (typo), so the section isn't a positioning context. Its decorative ::before glow (55% wide, right:-8%) is placed against the page and makes the page 1555px wide at 1440 and 875px at 810. html { overflow-x: hidden } hides this on desktop, but iPhones can still pan sideways. The same Embed's mobile media query is also missing its closing }.

Recommended solution in Webflow. Open the Embed in section-35 bottom-cta and change poition:relative; to position:relative; overflow:hidden;. Add the missing } at the end of the @media (max-width:768px) block. Publish, and the page returns to screen width.

G31 · Dead and overlapping code

Priority Low · Status 24 Sep Open

Problem.

  • (a) Four separate UTM mechanisms: site-head cookies (90 days), a site-footer localStorage copy that also rewrites the URL, page-footer localStorage plus .credolegal.com cookies, and Finsweet queryparam.
  • (b) Validators for elements that don't exist (#phone-number, #dob, #submitBtn, #msgphone), copied from another project (a comment still names softileo.info).
  • (c) Font Awesome CSS loaded with no icons used.
  • (d) A script that swaps "2025" for "2026" in the footer text.

Recommended solution in Webflow.

  1. Keep one UTM mechanism: the site-head block "7. UTM PERSISTENCE" (cookies, links, hidden fields), with fbclid fixed (G20), plus Finsweet for the hidden fields.
  2. Delete the site-footer localStorage script (Site settings → Custom code → Footer code) and the two UTM scripts in each page's footer code.
  3. Page settings → Custom code → Before body tag: delete the phone-number/dob formatter and the checkFormValidity block. The site head script already validates the form.
  4. Inside head tag: delete the Font Awesome <link>.
  5. Site settings → Custom code → Head code: delete block "4. FOOTER YEAR UPDATE", and type the year directly in the footer.

W1 · Move the shared page code to Site settings

Priority Low (workflow) · Status 24 Sep Recommendation

Problem. The 26 pages carry the same head and footer code (GTM, Mouseflow, Finsweet, Inputflow, the UTM and validation scripts, the heading-fix <style>, multi-step.js). Each fix above therefore needs 26 edits.

Recommended solution in Webflow. Copy the shared parts once into Site settings → Custom code (Head code / Footer code), then remove them from each page's Page settings → Custom code. Leave in page code only what differs per page: the canonical, og:url and the FAQPage schema.

A · Issues on every page — Fixed or no action needed

G1, G4, G5, G7 · Canonical, og:title/description, headings, titles — fixed

Status 24 Sep Fixed on the 26 pages with a prototype.

  • G1: each page's head code sets canonical and og:url to https://start.credolegal.com/<slug>, and there is exactly one canonical.
  • G4: og:title and og:description are set per page.
  • G5: one H1, 18 H2s, 35–37 H3s and 5–7 H4s per page. Section labels and headlines are H2, and card titles are H3.
  • G7: titles follow {H1} \| Credo Legal (taken from the prototype H1).

G8 · Firm name and address — fixed in the footer

Status 24 Sep Fixed in the footer (P.C., 1 Liberty Street, Suite 4010; BBB image alt says P.C.). The schema still says Florida: see G6.

G11, G12 · Trustpilot and hero image — fixed

Status 24 Sep Fixed. The Trustpilot widget renders "TrustScore 4.6 · 144 reviews" on desktop and mobile. The hero is an <img> with alt text, loading="eager" and responsive webp versions.

G15 · GA4 — partly confirmed

Status 24 Sep GA4 fires through GTM (requests to analytics.google.com and the server-side credoss.credolegal.com). Remaining, outside Webflow: in GTM, confirm that the form_submit key event fires only on a successful submit.

L7 · Tidio "failed to load" (cross-browser check; still present in the 24 Sep re-run) — no action

Status 24 Sep Not a fault. The Tidio script URL answers with a redirect (302) to a JavaScript file (200), and Tidio loaded normally in the separate 24 Sep browser test. The failure, which appears again in the 24 Sep re-run, comes from how the test browser handles that redirect.

G13, G14 · Withdrawn

G13 (missing hero eyebrow) and G14 (phone numbers) were withdrawn on 22 Sep: the eyebrow is hidden by design, and every tracked number matches the campaign phone sheet.

B. Page by page

For each page: the issues found only on that page, then the values that the general fixes need on it. The four pages without a prototype come first.

collection-defense

live · no prototype

Page-specific issues

  • Critical · Carries the payday page's content. The page shows the payday body (H1 "Payday Lender Suing You?", 20 payday mentions) with no prototype behind it, and takes ad traffic for the wrong intent. Its title is still "Let's Take The First Step To Help You Challenge Your Credit Card Debt". Then add a 301 in Site settings → Publishing → 301 redirects: /collection-defense → /debt-lawsuit-proof. Once a prototype exists, duplicate the nearest live page (Pages panel → ⋯ → Duplicate), give it this slug, and replace its copy.

debt-harassment-fdcpa-attorney

live · prototype

Page-specific issues

  • High · Rights section carries another vertical's lines. The six summary lines under the Rights citations are payday/lawsuit text ("Unlicensed lender = potential void…", "Improper service, mistake, excusable neglect…"). The citations above them are correct. In Webflow: in the Rights section, double-click each summary line and replace it with the prototype's text, top to bottom: "Up to $1,000 per lawsuit, paid by the collector." · "Emotional distress, job loss, medical expenses: all recoverable." · "Collector pays your legal fees when the claim succeeds." · "The violation alone is enough; no proven harm required." · "One year from the date of the violation, no extensions." · "Each call after a cease request: another $1,000 in play." Check before copying: the last line contradicts the first, since the FDCPA caps statutory damages at $1,000 per lawsuit (§ 1692k(a)(2)(A)). Confirm the wording with Credo before it goes live.

Suggested order of work

  1. Critical, Part B: redirect or rebuild the four payday-content pages (collection-defense, credit-cards, fcba-and-fdcpa, stop-wage-garnishment).
  2. G9, G10, G32: lead-data routing, the consent line and recorder masking (privacy and compliance).
  3. G19, G25, F1: mobile conversion blockers (sticky bar over Continue on 21 pages, number hidden in the menu, letter keyboard for the phone).
  4. L1, G20, G21: remove multi-step.js, capture fbclid, agree call tracking.
  5. G18 and the Part B copy fixes: citation case on all 30 pages, the fdcpa-attorney rights lines, the garnishment steps.
  6. The remaining Medium and Low items. Doing W1 and the Components step first turns most of them into single edits.

Review-hub comments (22 Sep)

Every row below is also a comment in the LP review hub, so the fixes can be worked and resolved in place. Open any prototype with ?review=1 and the sidebar lists them.

  • The 17 General rows are written as slot-portable comments (slot + scope: 'all') — the widget already supports this. Each is stored once (it appears once in "All pages") and shows on every prototype that declares the slot, in that page's "This page" list. They are anchored to the component each defect belongs to: hero H1 (canonical, duplicate domain, og, titles, imagery), hero lede (meta description), hero eyebrow (missing eyebrow), hero filler line (form endpoint, TCPA, form parity, phones), section headline (heading semantics), disclaimer (schema, NAP/entity), Trustpilot strip (dead widget) and the bottom CTA (GA4, CTA labels).
  • Page-specific rows are ordinary page-local comments on that page's own prototype.
  • The four broken pages have no prototype, so their row is logged as a Critical comment on the one-attorney prototype hero, which is the closest All-States page in the set.

Comment text follows one shape: LP AUDIT 22 Sep · <code/priority> · <scope>, then the defect (with the live URL), then the fix.
